# NETVAR_LOCAL - every dm32 field, class-labelled

lossless index of temp\dm32.txt. Regenerate, do not hand-edit.

`sz` is the field size in bytes, `t` the type id, `fl` the flag word,
`D` marks a row that also carries a desc pointer. `L####` is the line in
temp\dm32.txt, `f#` the field ordinal in its class. Values are STATIC -
nothing here was read from a running game.

Warnings: a name is only unique WITHIN a class (A2/A16). Rows at
`off=0x00000 sz=0x000` are reconstruction gaps, not real offsets (A5).
